# VFO Changelog — archived session history

> Split out of `SESSION_REFERENCE.md` on 2026-06-19 to keep the live hub lean.
>
> **Newest first. Nothing here is "current state" — it is how the system got here.** `SESSION_REFERENCE.md` keeps only the most recent ~3 sessions in its CURRENT STATE section plus the current value of each LIVE STATE field; everything older is archived here, verbatim.
>
> For the *current* per-handler reference, use `architecture/05-api-action-catalog.md` (every action + file), `architecture/01-system-map.md` (the daily crons), and the per-pipeline `flows/*` docs — not this file.

---

## Archived from SESSION_REFERENCE on 2026-07-23 (impl-bells-decision2-logo session)

- **Tax plans are now PROGRAM-SCOPED in the UI — a plan created under one program view (Holistic program 1 / VFO Tax Planning program 4) never renders under the other (incident-driven hardening)** (2026-07-23, branch `claude/tax-plan-program-scope` **FRONTEND repo ONLY**. **+0 actions (437)** — no backend edit; **backend UNTOUCHED** (live v645, `boldsign-webhook` v40, backend worktree clean → NO backend commit / NO backend PR). `npm run build` clean (546 modules). **NO migrations, NO DDL, NO schema change** → security advisor correctly NOT re-run. deno/parity/smoke/advisor all correctly N/A (FE-only, no backend, no dispatch, no DDL). Live DML data ops only (all user-approved — see below). **FRONTEND NOT deployed** — `npm run deploy` owed → next live-N (≥live-92). User click-through PASSED (per-program isolation both directions). Gotchas **#269** (ALWAYS-APPLIES, tax track) **+ #270** (diagnostic); **#123** cross-referenced (FE half now fixed). **MERGE-PENDING (FE repo only).**)
  - **1. THE INCIDENT.** During a user test run a tax plan created under the Holistic (program 1) view was then driven from the VFO Tax Planning (program 4) view — possible because `tax_load_plans` (`actions/tax/load-plans.ts`) returns a client's plans with NO program filter (root of gotcha #123) and `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx` rendered whatever came back under whichever program view was open. The program-4-only automation then half-worked: `automation_TAX_request_returns` stamps `tax_returns_requested_at` by plan id (program-agnostic — worked), but the upload path's `tax_returns_received_at` auto-stamp in `actions/vault/upload-notify.ts` is hard-gated to `program_id = 4` (silently skipped for the program-1 plan). Remediated by data ops (below), then the structural fix was built.
  - **2. THE FIX — one file, `src/components/admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`.** NEW module-scope helper `plansForProgram(plans, programId)` = `(plans||[]).filter(p => (p.program_id || 1) === (programId || 1))` (NULL/undefined `program_id` = legacy Holistic, program 1), applied at the SINGLE state entry point in `loadData()`: `setTaxPlans(scopedPlans)` + the per-plan progress loop iterates the scoped list. Everything downstream reads from `taxPlans`, so scoping there scopes plan cards, `livePlan` selection, TrackHero counts, MSM status, the track view, "+ Start Tax Plan" visibility, and deep-link resolution per program view. `refreshLivePlan()` inside `TaxPlanTrackView` is deliberately NOT filtered (it fetches the already-open plan by exact id). `ClientDetail.jsx:125` already `parseInt`s the `?program=` param, so the strict `===` is safe on admin/member/planner paths. `startPlan()` already passed `program_id` — unchanged. The BACKEND `program_id = 4` gate in `upload-notify.ts` was left AS-IS by design; the FE scoping is what keeps a cross-program plan from ever reaching it. Gotcha #269.
  - **3. CLEAN DATA CHECK (read-only).** All 8 existing `client_tax_plans` are clean — 3 program-1, 5 program-4, 0 NULL, 0 client-enrollment mismatches, no client with plans in BOTH programs — so the filter hides nothing from any real client. (Enrollment join: `client_tax_plans.client_id` → `clients` → `client_enrollments.enrollment_id` → `member_enrollments.program_id`.)
  - **4. LIVE DML DATA OPS (user-approved).** (a) **DURABLE — Tax Plan IQ account swap:** `strategic_member_groups` id 3 `stripe_account_id` `acct_1TtFVORul3PzixVa` → `acct_1Tg3NK2K2Sw793Ii` (user replaced a portal-created never-onboarded account with their real one; old account orphaned in Stripe; the durable `/payout-setup` link picks the new account at click time). (b) **Test fixtures (client 62 / member 59524, transient):** plan 61 (program 4, 7/22) wiped; the incident plan 62 CONVERTED program 1→4 in place + its 3 empty program-1 progress rows (tasks 83/84/85) deleted so the upload auto-stamp fired on re-upload; then ALL client-62 tax plans + children wiped (0 remain) for a clean re-test.
  - **5. $100 SANDBOX TRANSFERS — investigated, NOT our code (gotcha #270).** Three unexplained $100 sandbox transfers turned out to be Stripe's OWN sandbox demo data: `tr_3...`-style destination-charge transfers to freshly created demo connected accounts, no description/metadata, accounts in no VFO table. Verified the platform uses NO `transfer_data` / `on_behalf_of` anywhere (zero grep hits across `supabase/functions`) — every VFO payout is an explicit `POST /v1/transfers` with a memo. Harmless sandbox noise.
  - **Verification.** `npm run build` clean (546 modules); user click-through PASSED (both program views start empty with their own Start button after the fixture wipe; a plan created under one view does NOT appear under the other — isolation confirmed BOTH directions). FE-only → deno check / action parity / pipeline smoke gate / security advisor all correctly NOT required (no backend edit, no dispatch change, no DDL); action-count 437 + backend v645 cited unchanged from this hub. Files deliberately NOT touched: everything backend (`supabase/functions/*`, `boldsign-webhook` v40), `actions/tax/load-plans.ts` (the no-program-filter loader stays), `actions/vault/upload-notify.ts` (the `program_id = 4` gate stays by design).

## Archived from SESSION_REFERENCE on 2026-07-23 (planner-phase-notes session)

- **Durable Stripe Connect payout-setup links — the 4 "Set Up Payment Details" emails now embed a `/payout-setup?token=` page that mints a FRESH Stripe account link per click (single-use raw `connect.stripe.com` links retired)** (2026-07-23, branch `claude/vfo-session-setup-be671e` BOTH repos. **+1 action (437)** [`connect_setup_link`, PUBLIC], deno 0, `npm run build` clean (546 modules), `boldsign-webhook` untouched (v40). **1 migration applied live + committed** (`20260723100000_connect_setup_tokens.sql`); **security advisor GREEN** after it (5× `rls_enabled_no_policy` INFO + `pg_net` WARN accepted); anon probe on `connect_setup_tokens` → `*/0`. **Backend DEPLOYED ONCE → live v645** (this deploy ALSO shipped the prior session's owed v644 delta — the Continue-stalled bell title + the `TAX_PLANNER` token wiring — now RESOLVED; the registry's v644 is unattributed/likely a parallel session, live-verified value is v645). **NO pending backend delta. FRONTEND NOT deployed** — `npm run deploy` owed → next live-N (also carries all prior sessions' owed FE). Pipeline smoke gate **5/5 user-run vs v645**. User click-through PASSED end-to-end. Gotcha **#268** (ALWAYS-APPLIES). **MERGE-PENDING both repos.**)
  - **1. THE PROBLEM.** Stripe account-onboarding links (`connect.stripe.com/setup/...`) are SINGLE-USE and EXPIRE. The 4 "Set Up Payment Details" emails (members, specialists, strategic groups, tax planning groups) each embedded one raw link, so a second click dead-ended on the portal landing page (a bare `PORTAL_BASE` refresh/return_url refresh).
  - **2. THE FIX — durable token page.** NEW table `connect_setup_tokens` holds ONE permanent token per payout entity (`entity_type` ∈ member/specialist/strategic_group/tax_planning_group + `entity_key`, `unique(entity_type, entity_key)`, deny-all RLS). NEW `utils/connect-setup-token.ts`: `ensureConnectSetupToken` (lazily mints one durable token per entity, reuses `token32()` from `gmail-draft.ts`, insert-race-safe — re-selects the winner on a unique-collision) + `connectSetupUrl(token)` → `${PORTAL_BASE}/payout-setup?token=<64-hex>`. The 4 creators (`actions/members/stripe-connect-request.ts`, `actions/specialists/stripe-connect-request.ts`, `actions/strategic/group-stripe-connect-request.ts`, `actions/tax-planners/group-stripe-connect-request.ts`) drop their inline `account_link` mint block and email the durable URL instead; the sandbox toggle + Stripe key reads are KEPT (still needed for the account-CREATION branch), and the now-unused `PORTAL_BASE` import was dropped from each creator.
  - **3. NEW PUBLIC action `connect_setup_link`** (`actions/payouts/connect-setup-link.ts`, registered in `PUBLIC_HANDLERS`, +1 dispatch entry): token → entity row → `stripe_account_id` → reads the shared "MAP 1" `pipeline_sandbox_config` `sandbox_mode` toggle → mints a FRESH Stripe `account_link` (`type=account_onboarding`) on every click. `refresh_url` = the same durable `/payout-setup?token=` page (so a mid-flow Stripe expiry loops back and re-mints), `return_url` = `${PORTAL_BASE}/payout-setup?done=1`. Friendly generic errors (400 no token / 404 invalid token or entity / 410 "no longer valid" on a Stripe error — e.g. a sandbox-created account read under the live key); the real Stripe error goes to `console.log` WITHOUT the token (only `entity_type`/`entity_key`/account/mode logged — the token is a durable bearer secret, deliberately kept out of logs); fire-and-forget `last_used_at` stamp that never blocks the redirect.
  - **4. FRONTEND public page.** NEW `src/pages/PayoutSetupPage.jsx` (mirrors `ConnectCardPage`: `TokenShell`, same `API_URL` fetch shape): auto-POSTs `connect_setup_link`, redirects to the returned Stripe URL; `?done=1` → "Payment details submitted" card; error → friendly card + "reply to the setup email" hint. `src/App.jsx` adds the `/payout-setup` route next to `/connect-card`. No `theme.js` change (public pages default light).
  - **Verification.** `deno check` 0 errors; `npm run build` clean (546 modules); action parity 437 (431 dispatch + 6 logins); security advisor GREEN post-migration; anon probe on `connect_setup_tokens` → `*/0`; pipeline smoke gate 5/5 (user-run vs v645); user click-through PASSED (resend ICG setup email → draft carries the durable link; the localhost `/payout-setup?token=` link worked on EVERY repeat click with a fresh Stripe page each time; a sandbox Connect account under the live platform correctly showed the friendly error card; the `?done=1` and no-token cards both render). OWED to user: send/delete the test Gmail drafts from testing (ICG ×2 + Test Group). Files deliberately NOT touched: `boldsign-webhook/*` (v40), `router/webhooks.ts pipeRow`.

## Archived from SESSION_REFERENCE on 2026-07-23 (Request-additional-info session)

- **Tax Planner portal step restrictions (per-step visible-but-locked whitelist) + 6 planner notification bells + Tax 4 "Client decision 1" made CLICK-ONLY (24h auto-lock removed) + `TAX_PLANNER` email CC chip + planner premature-payout bug fix + assorted fixes** (2026-07-22, branch `claude/vfo-session-setup-a13fde` BOTH repos. **+0 actions (436)**, deno 0, `boldsign-webhook` untouched (v40). **1 migration applied live + committed** (`20260722130000_tax_planner_step_notifications.sql`); **security advisor GREEN** after it (5× `rls_enabled_no_policy` INFO + `pg_net` WARN accepted). **Backend DEPLOYED 3× → live v643** (v641/v642/v643); the **ONE pending delta (v644)** — the Continue-stalled bell title + the `TAX_PLANNER` token wiring on ~25 `resolveTemplateRecipients` sites + `email-recipients.ts` — SHIPPED inside the next session's v645 deploy (now RESOLVED); its FE rode `live-91`/`live-92`. Pipeline smoke gate **OWED user-run** vs the final deployed version. User click-through PASSED end-to-end on everything. Gotchas **#262–#267** (#262/#264/#265 ALWAYS-APPLIES).)
  - **1. Planner per-step lock — three-surface editable whitelist (gotcha #262).** In `plannerMode` the Tax Priorities tab now shows every step but LOCKS all but a whitelisted subset (outer div `cursor:not-allowed` + inner div `pointerEvents:'none'`; `+ Add Specialist` and the specialist repeater picker stay active). The editable subset is decided in THREE synced places: FE `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` (12 names, `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`), BE `PLANNER_EDITABLE_TASK_NAMES` (11 `tax_save_task`-reachable names, exported from `save-task.ts` — a planner caller who touches any other task via `tax_save_task` gets 403 "Tax planners cannot edit this step"), and the TRIMMED `TAX_PLANNER_ALLOWED_ACTIONS` (`role-gates.ts` — removed decision/readyfortax3/presentation_schedule/request_returns/pricing/extrameeting/depositrefund/sendagreement/save_deposit_pi/allocate_planner/msm_update_tax_status). Fixed a leak where two child styles set `pointerEvents:'auto'` (re-enabling clicks inside the lock) → now conditional `'none'`/`undefined` (gotcha #263). Also: `tax5bUnlocked` (Post Allocation) now requires a specialist Confirm-ready status of `Yes`/`Undecided` (a lone `No` no longer unlocks); assess_form step restyled; TrackHero live/stopped toggle non-clickable in plannerMode; `tax_3_decision` confirm buttons alert-and-return if no ATP allocated.
  - **2. SIX planner notification bells (NEW `utils/tax-planner-notify.ts`).** `notifyAllocatedPlanner(sb, ruleKey, plan, {...})` resolves `tax_planners.email` from `plan.tax_planner_id`, links `/tax-planner/client/<client_id>?program=<pid||1>`, try/catch-safe, skips null client/planner/email. The six: **TAX_planner_allocated** (allocate-planner.ts, on genuine (re)assignment — reads prior id first), **TAX_planner_returns_uploaded** (vault/upload-notify.ts, to the latest plan-with-planner's ATP on client tax-return upload — both token page + portal paths), **TAX_planner_hlm_ready** (invoice-receipt.ts, after retainer receipt completes), **TAX_planner_decision2_ready** (revshare.ts, after retainer revshare success), **TAX_planner_impl_decision_ready** (save-task.ts, when "Confirm ready for implementation" → "Yes"), **TAX_planner_post_meeting** (revshare-sweep.ts, day-after-meeting nudge — `tax4_meeting_date < today` + planner set + `tax4_planner_nudge_sent_at` null + NOT(presentation done AND post_review decision set), one-shot stamp, rule-disable-gated BEFORE stamping per gotcha #176).
  - **3. Tax 4 "Client decision 1" is CLICK-ONLY — 24h auto-lock REMOVED (gotcha #264).** `revshare-sweep.ts`: Tax 4 no longer writes `'Auto-Locked'` (the value survives only as historical data + the untouched Tax 5 implementation twin). Retainer revshare fires ONLY from the client's Confirmed/Proceed click. Replaced with a Continue reminder ladder mirroring the Undecided one — 48h reminder-email tier (rule `TAX_postreview_continue_reminder_email`, reuses `TAX_postreview|Reminder` template) + 96h PF-bell tier (rule `TAX_postreview_continue_stalled`, title "«Client name» hasn't made a decision on Client decision 1") — REUSING the `post_review_reminder_sent_at`/`post_review_pf_notified_at` guard columns (safe: Continue/Undecided mutually exclusive per plan). FE Continue sub-steps rewritten click-only (no 24h language).
  - **4. CRITICAL money fix — planner premature-payout (gotcha #265).** The v633 planner-payout "stranded leg" retry paid the planner share on ANY freshly paid plan (receipt set + planner + share + `planner_paid` NULL) BEFORE the client's Tax 4 confirmation — masked for months by the then-24h auto-lock. Now a NULL planner leg is only retried when the MEMBER leg resolved (`retainer_rev_paid`/`implementation_rev_paid` ∈ Yes / Money Mapping / N/A — No Share Due); Failed-leg retries unchanged. Live-verified fixed on v642.
  - **5. `TAX_PLANNER` email role token (gotcha #266).** Added `"TAX_PLANNER"` to `RECIPIENT_ROLE_TOKENS` (`email-recipients.ts`) + `ROLE_LABELS.TAX_PLANNER='Tax Planner'` (`templateMeta.js`) so admins can drop a "Tax Planner" Cc chip on any tax email template. Resolves per-send from the plan's `tax_planner_id` (helper `taxPlannerEmail`); ALL 25 tax `resolveTemplateRecipients` sites now pass it in ctx. Sandbox suppresses all Cc, so the chip is only observable live. Also: `ready-for-tax3.ts` confirm sends now 400 unless a planner is allocated ("Allocate an Advanced Tax Planner before sending the Tax 3 confirmation email"; declined exempt); `[Planner Name]` token substitution added across the confirm/HLM/decision handlers.
  - **6. Notification bell render/click fixes (gotcha #267).** `NotificationBell.jsx`: row-click now marks-read + removes locally BEFORE navigating (the destination page mounts its own bell whose load raced the write and resurrected the row — affected admin too); "View all" hidden for `tax_planner` sessions; dropdown widened 380→480px, titles wrap. `TaxPlannerPortal.jsx`: removed the tab strip's `position:relative; zIndex:100` (it painted over the bell dropdown's header strip).
  - **7. Assorted.** "Jake Latham" → `jlatham@elitert.com` added as a permanent new PF option in all three PF maps (`pf-emails.ts` + `tax-notify.ts` + `constants/map1-pfs.ts`) + the `ClientDetail.jsx` Assigned PF dropdown. `client_tax_plans.tax4_planner_nudge_sent_at` (one-shot nudge guard) added by the migration.
  - **Verification.** `deno check` 0 errors; action parity 436 (430 dispatch + 6 logins); security advisor GREEN post-migration; user click-through PASSED end-to-end (planner 13-step lock incl. the pointerEvents leak fix; unlock gate No/Undecided/Yes; all 6 planner bells fired in sandbox; Continue new copy + 48h reminder + 96h PF bell via backdated sweeps; NO auto-lock — decision stayed NULL until click; planner payout paid at the correct moment post-fix, premature-payout bug reproduced pre-fix then verified fixed on v642; bell dropdown render + one-click clearing). ## Archived from SESSION_REFERENCE on 2026-07-23 (tax-plan-program-scope session)

- **Tax phase-numbering alignment (badges match titles; the two "Tax 5" phases merged into ONE card) + MAP 1 risk-profile carry-over onto new Holistic tax plans** (2026-07-22, branch `claude/vfo-session-setup-639cca` BOTH repos. **+0 actions (436)**, deno 0, `npm run build` clean (546 modules), `boldsign-webhook` untouched (v40). **NO migrations, NO schema change** → security advisor correctly NOT re-run. **Backend DEPLOYED ONCE → live v640**; **NO pending backend delta**. **FRONTEND NOT deployed** — `npm run deploy` owed → ≥live-89, with **no** deploy-time DB ops attached. Pipeline smoke gate **5/5 user-run vs v640**. User click-through PASSED on both halves. Gotchas **#260–#261** (#260 ALWAYS-APPLIES on the tax track). **MERGE-PENDING both repos.**)
  - **1. THE PROBLEM.** The tax track's phase badge was a positional index while the card title carried a business number, so they only agreed by luck. Holistic (program 1, 7 stored phases) drifted at the tail — `Tax 5 (Post Allocation)` badged 6, `Tax 6 - Implementation` badged 7. VFO Tax Planning (program 4, 8 stored phases) was worse: an unnumbered `Set Up` phase took badge 1, so `Tax 1` badged 2 and EVERY row below was off by one, ending with `Tax 6` badged 8. Root cause is structural: both programs store **two** phases named `Tax 5` (`… (Specialist Allocation)` / `… (Post Allocation)`).
  - **2. THE FIX (display-only — no phase renames).** Renaming the phases was rejected: the names are exact-string-matched in `actions/clients/overview-tax.ts` `phaseReach`, at ~14 sites in `TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`, and are stored as DATA in `client_notes.phase_name`. Instead: NEW module-level `phaseBadgeToken(name)` (`Set Up` → `'S'`, `/^Tax (\d+)/` → the digit) + `phaseShortLabel(name)` (the descriptive half) in `components/admin/tax/TaxPrioritiesTab.jsx`, and the **two Tax 5 phases now render as ONE card** badged `5`, titled "Tax 5 - Education & DD", with "Specialist Allocation" and "Post Allocation" as sub-sections inside — each keeping its own status pill, the per-specialist repeater, the `+ Add Specialist` button, and the Post-Allocation unlock gate. Badges now read `1,2,3,4,5,6` (Holistic) / `S,1,2,3,4,5,6` (Tax Planning), matching titles exactly. `components/shared/TrackKit.jsx` `PhaseStepper` gained an optional per-step `number` (falls back to `i + 1`, so all 11 other track callers are untouched), letting the merged card occupy ONE stepper node; stepper labels switched to the descriptive half (`Diagnostic`, `ROI Meeting`, `Education & DD`…) since the node already carries the number. Shared `PhasePill` extracted (3 call sites). Hero task counts deliberately UNCHANGED (already per-task, not per-card).
  - **3. ONE notes thread on the merged card.** `components/shared/PhaseNotes.jsx` `PhaseNotesPanel` gained an optional **`phaseNames` ARRAY** prop that widens what is READ while `phaseName` still governs what is WRITTEN. The Tax 5 card renders a single Notes button in the standard phase-header spot (left of the status pill), count = both phases summed, reading `[TAX5A_PHASE, TAX5B_PHASE]` and writing under `TAX5A_PHASE`. Consequence to know: every NEW Tax 5 note persists with `phase_name = 'Tax 5 - Education & DD (Specialist Allocation)'` whichever half it concerns, and shows that label as its chip in the client Profile "All Notes" list (the only other `phase_name` consumer, display-only). All other `PhaseNotesPanel` callers default to the old single-phase behaviour.
  - **4. MAP 1 risk carry-over (Holistic only, +0 actions).** `actions/tax/start-plan.ts` now seeds a newly created Holistic plan's "Client risk profile complete" task from the client's MAP 1 "Priorities Decided / Risk Profile" answer, mapping `Completed + Risk N` → `Yes — Risk N — <Mindset>` (verbatim `status_options` strings; Risk 2's second separator is a plain hyphen, not an em dash). Both tasks resolved BY NAME within program 1, not by hardcoded ids 13/83. Fires at plan CREATION only (a later MAP 1 correction does NOT retro-update existing plans); `Completed + N/A` seeds nothing; Holistic-only (`!program_id || program_id === 1`); fully try/catch-wrapped so any miss leaves the field blank rather than failing plan creation. The seeded value stays an editable dropdown. Side effect to expect: a new Holistic plan now opens at `1 / N` with `Tax 1 - Diagnostic` reading "In progress", because the seed stamps `completed_date` = today + `completed_by` = "Auto — from MAP 1 risk profile".
  - **5. Test-fixture cleanup.** Test Client (id 62, member 59524) `client_tax_plans` rows **57 + 59 DELETED** at the user's request (5 `client_tax_progress` + 1 `client_tax_specialists` children too); 0 tax plans remain. Plan 57 was a program_id=4 row on a Holistic client left over from the prior session — its progress rows pointed at program-4 task ids invisible under program-1 phases, which is what made the risk-seed appear to have failed during testing.
  - **Verification.** `deno check` 0 errors; `npm run build` clean (546 modules); action parity 436 (430 dispatch + 6 logins); pipeline smoke gate 5/5 (user-run vs v640); user click-through PASSED (numbering on both programs, merged card expand/collapse, specialist add, Post-Allocation unlock, single notes thread, risk seed confirmed in-DB as `Yes — Risk 2 - Moderately Conservative Mindset` on a fresh plan). Files deliberately NOT touched: `boldsign-webhook/*` (v40), `router/webhooks.ts`, all phase NAMES in `program_client_phases`.

**CURRENT STATE entries evicted (kept newest ~3 in the hub):**

- **Agreement-templates → PDF → Gmail-draft tool (`agreement_pdf_draft`)** (2026-07-15, branch `claude/tmp-html-pdf-draft` — edge code + vfo-react docs; backend deployed **v606** as interim `tmp_agreement_pdf_draft`, v607 rename deployed + tagged between sessions; NO DB, NO template edits, NO FE; **+1 action → 413**; deno 0). Superadmin-only standalone utility — NOT in any pipeline/chain. Built at the user's request and kept (was originally a throwaway).
  - **What it does.** Body `{ action:"agreement_pdf_draft", token, template_ids:number[], to?, subject?, body? }` → loads those `agreement_templates` rows, renders each `html_body` **verbatim** to PDF via `api.html2pdf.app` (reuses the existing `HTML2PDF_API_KEY` + the advisor/accountant invoice-receipt render pattern), then drops **ONE** Gmail draft with all PDFs attached into the configured Gmail account's Drafts (`utils/gmail-draft.ts` `getGmailAccessToken`+`draftGmail`). Returns `{ok,draftId,count,rendered:[{id,title,filename}],missing}`. Filenames from `title` (sanitized, `.pdf`, deduped by id). `[placeholders]` render as-is — raw template, not a filled agreement. Defaults: `to`=jlatham@elitert.com, `subject`="Agreement PDFs".
  - **File:** `actions/agreements/pdf-draft.ts` (export `agreementPdfDraft`) + 1 import + 1 AUTH_HANDLERS entry in `router/dispatch.ts`. Self-checks `auth.isSuperadmin` → 403 otherwise (the gate; not added to SUPERADMIN_ONLY_ACTIONS).
  - **Invoke without a pasted token (reusable technique).** Call the live edge fn server-side from Postgres via `pg_net` so the admin token never leaves the DB: `select net.http_post(url:=<fn-url>, body:=jsonb_build_object('action','agreement_pdf_draft','token',(select token from admin_sessions where email='jlatham@elitert.com' and expires_at>now() order by expires_at desc limit 1),'template_ids',jsonb_build_array(...)), headers:='{"Content-Type":"application/json"}'::jsonb, timeout_milliseconds:=120000)` → then read `net._http_response where id=<request_id>` (async; poll). `verify_jwt=false` so no anon key needed. `template_ids` MUST be a JSON array (single-element PowerShell arrays collapse to a scalar under `ConvertTo-Json` → use `jsonb_build_array` / build the array text by hand).

- **VFO Specialist Revenue — recurring monthly payment plans** (2026-07-09, branch `claude/vfo-recurring-specialist-revenue-67e23f` BOTH repos; **backend DEPLOYED v563→v569** (7 approved deploys); **frontend dev-only, NOT deployed → next `live-N`**; DB migration applied via MCP + committed; advisor GREEN; smoke 5/5 (user-run, v563 + v564); **full sandbox E2E PASS** incl. Stripe month-by-month simulation). Multi-agent session (Fable planned/verified, Opus executed). New gotchas #197–#199.
  - **A. Feature.** Third New-Payment method **"Recurring monthly — send ACH setup link"**: plan (recipient-split snapshot + charge day **1st–20th**) + a DEDICATED Stripe customer per plan + setup email `SPECREV_recurring_setup` linking `/specialist-revenue-pay?token=<setup_token>&kind=recurring`. Specialist authorizes ONCE via Checkout `mode=subscription` (ACH-only, at par); the first charge is deferred to the next charge-day occurrence via `subscription_data[billing_cycle_anchor]` + `proration_behavior=none` — NOT `trial_end`, which renders "31 days free" (gotcha #197; 17:00 UTC preferred, 00:05 UTC fallback under Stripe's next-natural-billing-date limit, hard clamp, session `expires_at` 1h, `custom_text` no-charge-today note; a charge day equal to today with >2h lead charges TODAY). Activation chains a one-time `SPECREV_recurring_setup_confirmation` email (guard `confirmation_email_sent_at`).
  - **B. Monthly cycle = normal `specialist_revenue_requests` rows.** `invoice.finalized` pre-creates the month's request row as `processing` (labeled **"Pending"** + grey "Recurring" pill; requires `invoice.finalized` on the Stripe endpoints — **user added it to sandbox + live 2026-07-09**; the paid processor self-heals row creation if it's missing). Invoice-paid events (`invoice.paid`/`invoice.payment_succeeded`/`invoice_payment.paid`) funnel through `processSpecrevRecurringInvoicePaid` (subscription-match guard, atomic `claim_specrev_recurring_invoice`, find-or-create by `stripe_invoice_id`) → `received` (**"Completed"**) → chains the EXISTING `specialist_revenue_invoicereceipt` + `specialist_revenue_payout` untouched; deals repeat every cycle (Reconciliation accumulates by design). The $0 `subscription_create` stub invoice is skipped (gotcha #198). `invoice.payment_failed` → row `failed` + `SPECREV_recurring_payment_failed_bell` (Jake + Tracy, dismissible), plan STAYS active (Stripe retries). Cancel (new page's button, or a Stripe-side delete via `customer.subscription.deleted`) stops future months immediately; an in-flight monthly ACH still completes + pays out. `next_billing_date` derived across Stripe API versions (gotcha #199).
  - **C. Surfaces.** +6 actions (→381): `specialist_revenue_recurring_{create,load,cancel}` (AUTH superadmin, in `TAB_ACTIONS.accounting`) + `{pay_load,checkout,confirmationemail}` (PUBLIC). New page **Accounting → Specialists → VFO Specialist Recurring Revenue Payments** (`SpecialistRecurringPanel.jsx`, nav key `specialist_recurring`) listing plans (status pills Setup pending / Active / Canceled, next-billing, expandable recipient split) with confirm-dialog Cancel. The house-account `pending` = "Awaiting bank transfer" label is untouched. Recurring rides the shared **MAP 1** sandbox row like all SPECREV code.

- **One Page Growth Plan — decimal sub-task numbering** (2026-07-08, branch `claude/growth-subtask-decimal-numbering`, `vfo-react` ONLY; **frontend SHIPPED as `live-47-growth-subtask-numbering`** 2026-07-08 12:41; no backend/DB/edge change). *(Moved from SESSION_REFERENCE CURRENT STATE 2026-07-09.)* Fixed the One Page Plan renumbering complaint: adding a sub-task used to bump every later item because numbering was a flat 1..N across tasks+subs (`grouping.js orderOnePage` ended with `flat.map((a,i)=>({num:i+1}))`). Now top-level tasks keep whole numbers (1, 2, 3…) and each sub-task takes its parent's number + a decimal (`1.1`, `1.2`), so adding `1.1` never renumbers task 2. `num` is now a **string** — used only for display (matrix dot + table `#` cell; no arithmetic anywhere). Orphan sub-tasks (parent completed/removed) still fall back to a plain whole number on their own line. `MatrixDot` shrinks its font for 3+ char labels so decimals fit the 34px dot. Files: `src/components/growth/{grouping.js,GrowthOnePage.jsx}`. No new action; action count / deno baseline / versions all unchanged.

- **Specialist $99/mo license invoice/receipt + Stage 4→5 automation fix** (2026-07-07, branch `claude/specialist-lic-invoice-fix` — backend only, `vfo-edge-functions`; **backend DEPLOYED v545→v550 across 6 approved deploys**, save-point tag **`backend-good-2026-07-07-v551`**; **DB applied live via MCP; advisor GREEN**; no frontend change). *(Moved from SESSION_REFERENCE CURRENT STATE 2026-07-08. Independently reviewed + hardened 2026-07-08 → v561; the claim RPC's migration file `20260708130000_claim_specialist_license_invoice_rpc.sql` was committed then — gotcha #196.)*
  - **Symptom.** Jake reported: specialist pays the $99/mo license via subscription checkout, gets the confirmation email, but the invoice/receipt email never drafts and the record never advances Stage 4→5 — a flow that had "worked ~30 times before." Stripe showed the webhook returning 200; nothing in the DB moved.
  - **Bug 1 — Stripe API version fragility (real, but not the root cause).** The `invoice.paid`/`invoice.payment_succeeded` handler hard-gated on the invoice's subscription ref (`invoice.subscription || invoice.parent?.subscription_details?.subscription`); the account's newer API version (`2025-08-27.basil`) can omit both, so the guard silently skipped the whole block. Fixed: never gate on the event's subscription ref — fall back to the row's stored `lic_subscription_id`. ALSO made the first invoice/receipt + Stage advance fire inline from `checkout.session.completed` (card path) by expanding the subscription's `latest_invoice` at checkout time, so the card-payment case no longer depends on a separate invoice event arriving/parsing correctly at all (ACH still waits for the later event, since funds aren't confirmed yet at checkout time).
  - **Bug 2 — THE actual root cause: a PostgREST schema-cache anomaly + a silently-swallowed error.** Direct REST calls (curl GET/PATCH) against `specialist_onboarding` resolved `lic_last_invoice_id` (added 2026-06-05) fine; the SAME write issued by the deployed edge function via `supabase-js` reliably threw `42703 column does not exist` — before AND after a `NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema'`. The ORIGINAL code destructured only `{ data }` from the claim UPDATE, so this real DB error was invisible — the handler just silently returned, looking identical to "nothing to do." This is almost certainly why it's been failing quietly for a while, not "just started" — the visible signal (confirmation email, a DIFFERENT code path) kept firing. **Fix:** every `supabase.from(...)` call in the license-invoice path now checks `error` and logs explicitly (gotcha #187); the specific troublesome write was moved into a new `SECURITY DEFINER` RPC `claim_specialist_license_invoice(p_onboarding_id, p_invoice_id, p_paid_at)` called via `supabase.rpc(...)` — RPC only needs PostgREST to resolve the function name (a coarser, stable cache entry), so the write now runs as native SQL with full column visibility, sidestepping the anomaly entirely (gotcha #188).
  - **Verification.** End-to-end on a fresh test specialist with a real sandbox card payment (`4242…`): invoice/receipt drafted (`INV-SPECLIC-…`/`REC-SPECLIC-…`), Stage 4→5 advanced automatically, claimed exactly once (idempotency intact — the `.or(is.null,neq)` dedup pattern, gotcha #28, is preserved inside the new RPC). Two rounds of Stripe-event resend testing plus one full fresh-specialist real-payment test were used to isolate the two bugs from each other. deno check 0; action count 373 (unchanged); advisor GREEN; pipeline smoke **5/5 PASS**.
  - **Cleanup.** A temporary diagnostic table (`_webhook_debug_trace`) + matching trace-log calls were added mid-session to see past the log-visibility gap, then fully removed once the fix was confirmed — zero trace remains. Two test `specialist_onboarding` rows (created for verification) were fully deleted along with their progress/ledger/notification/document-number rows. *(2026-07-08 note: one orphan `specialist_license_payments` row for that test survived this cleanup and was removed the next day.)*
  - **New gotchas #187–#188.** No config remaining — this was a pure bugfix, fully verified live.

- **VFO Specialist Revenue — shared house-account "record expected payment → mark received" model** (2026-07-07, branch `claude/specrev-house-account` BOTH repos; **backend DEPLOYED v554→v555**; frontend **SHIPPED `live-44-specialist-revenue-house-account`** (react #102 / edge #75); **DB applied live via MCP; advisor GREEN**). Replaced the rotating per-request "Bank transfer" option (built the day before, `live-40`) with a single permanent receiving account. *(Moved from SESSION_REFERENCE CURRENT STATE 2026-07-08.)*
  - **A. The model.** ONE shared VFO Stripe "house" customer PER MODE (sandbox/live), reused forever, giving a permanent ACH account/routing via Stripe `funding_instructions` handed to every specialist to push to. Stored in NEW table `specialist_revenue_house_account` (sandbox boolean PK; deny-all RLS). An earlier per-specialist `specialist_revenue_accounts` table was created then dropped same-session (no residue; migration file keeps that name but creates the house table).
  - **B. Flow.** Admin picks "Bank transfer — record expected payment" → `send-request.ts` (`payment_method:'pending'`) calls NEW `ensureHouseAccount` (`house-account.ts`), inserts request `payment_status='pending'` + lines, sends **NO email**, returns the house account details. Later admin clicks "Mark payment received" → NEW action `specialist_revenue_confirm_received` (`confirm-received.ts`, self-gates `auth.isSuperadmin`): trust-the-click, creates+confirms a `customer_balance` PaymentIntent to draw the amount from the pooled house cash balance.
  - **C. The critical guard.** If the draw doesn't immediately succeed the PI is **CANCELED** and the action returns `{ok:false,not_funded:true,message}` — otherwise the open PI sits and silently swallows a later transfer into the pool. On success the request flips to `received` + chains `specialist_revenue_invoicereceipt` + `specialist_revenue_payout` via service-role. The confirm PI carries **NO `metadata.pipeline`** (only `request_id` + `payment_kind=specialist_revenue_house`) so `router/webhooks.ts` ignores it — **webhooks.ts NOT touched this session**.
  - **D. Received-only surfaces (latent over-count fix).** `SpecialistReconciliationPanel.jsx` now counts only `payment_status='received'` requests; `SpecialistRevenuePanel.jsx` viewer money totals + By-member rollup are RECEIVED-ONLY (`receivedFiltered`) — pending/requested is expected money, not reconciled revenue. By-specialist list still shows all.
  - **Files.** Backend (`supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/`): `actions/specialist-revenue/{load,send-request,confirm-received,house-account}.ts` + `router/dispatch.ts` + migration `20260707000000_specialist_revenue_accounts.sql`. Frontend (`src/components/admin/`): `SpecialistPaymentInput.jsx`, `specialistRevenueShared.jsx` (exports shared `MarkReceivedButton`), `SpecialistRevenueAutomationPanel.jsx`, `SpecialistRevenuePanel.jsx`, `SpecialistReconciliationPanel.jsx`.
  - **Deploys.** Backend deployed twice with approval → **v555 live** (v554 feature + v555 PI-cancel fix). `boldsign-webhook` untouched (v40). Frontend since SHIPPED as **`live-44-specialist-revenue-house-account`**.
  - **E2E (sandbox, all passed, test data deleted).** pending save + house-account creation; not-funded click (safe block + PI auto-cancel); funded → received → invoice/receipt + Connect payout; Money-Mapping recipient (notice email, no transfer); ACH-debit link control (unchanged, full webhook flow); reconciliation + viewer received-only verified. deno check 0; action count **374**; pipeline smoke **5/5 PASS** (post-v555, user-run); advisor GREEN.

(The `checkout.ts`/`pay-load.ts` bank_transfer guards were left as a harmless defensive 400 — no longer reachable but not dead-weight.)

- **VFO Specialist Revenue — "Bank transfer" payment option** (2026-07-06, branch `claude/musing-turing-b48e4b` BOTH repos; backend v541→v542, frontend shipped **`live-40-specrev-bank-transfer`**; DB via MCP; advisor GREEN). *(Moved from SESSION_REFERENCE CURRENT STATE 2026-07-07.)* ⚠️ **This rotating per-request model was SUPERSEDED the next day by the shared house-account model** (`live-44`, gotcha #189) and its bank-transfer webhook/UI/columns/templates were REMOVED 2026-07-07 (v556, branch `claude/specrev-cleanup-deadcode`) — the description below is historical.
  - **A. Per-request payment-method pick.** New Payment (`SpecialistPaymentInput.jsx`) offered a radio-card selector: **ACH-debit payment link** (existing default — unchanged) OR **Bank transfer** (new). Bank transfer: `actions/specialist-revenue/send-request.ts` created a confirmed Stripe `customer_balance` PaymentIntent (`funding_type=bank_transfer`, `us_bank_transfer`, metadata `pipeline=VFO_SPECIALIST_REVENUE` + `request_id` + `payment_kind=specialist_revenue`); Stripe issued per-customer **virtual bank details** (account/routing/bank name/reference/hosted URL) persisted on the request row (6 `bank_transfer_*` columns) and emailed to the specialist via a pay-link-free template (`SPECREV_payment_request_banktransfer`). Specialist pushed an ACH credit / wire from their own bank. On PI-creation failure send-request **deleted the just-inserted request row** and returned the Stripe error. `checkout.ts` + `pay-load.ts` **refused** bank-transfer tokens (400) so the link path couldn't double-charge.
  - **B. Status design — deliberately NO 'processing' stage.** The request stayed `payment_status='requested'` through funding so the payout-sweep 48h/96h reminder ladder (keyed on `'requested'`) kept running; `payout-sweep.ts` gained per-method 48h reminder branches (bank-transfer rows used `SPECREV_payment_reminder_banktransfer`, restating the stored bank details). When funds FULLY arrived, `payment_intent.succeeded` (webhook guard widened to accept `requested` + bank_transfer) flipped it straight to **`received`** and chained invoice/receipt + member payout exactly like ACH (`invoice-receipt.ts` + `utils/specialist-revenue-invoice.ts` gained bank-transfer label branches — invoice "Bank Transfer", receipt "Via Bank Transfer" no last4, "[PROCESSING_TIME]" → "Your bank transfer has been received.").
  - **C. No confirmation email for bank transfer** (Jake's decision — the invoice/receipt email IS the confirmation; `confirmation-email.ts` untouched).
  - **D. Partial-funding bell + UI.** An additive `payment_intent.partially_funded` webhook block (`router/webhooks.ts`) → dismissible FYI bell to Jake via `notifyByRule('SPECREV_partial_funding_bell')` + persisted received-so-far dollars to `bank_transfer_amount_received`, shown as an orange "received so far" note in both the Revenue viewer and the Automation tracker (shared `RequestRow` in `specialistRevenueShared.jsx`, which also rendered the copy-buttoned bank-details block). ⚠️ On a `customer_balance` PI, Stripe's `amount_received` stays 0 until FULLY funded — the partial total was derived as `amount - next_action.display_bank_transfer_instructions.amount_remaining` (real bug caught in testing → **gotcha #184**; the v542 deploy fixed this).
  - **Files.** Backend (all `supabase/functions/vfo-admin-api/`): `actions/specialist-revenue/{send-request,checkout,pay-load,payout-sweep,invoice-receipt}.ts` + `utils/specialist-revenue-invoice.ts` + `router/webhooks.ts` + 2 migrations. Frontend (`src/components/admin/`): `SpecialistPaymentInput.jsx`, `specialistRevenueShared.jsx`, `EmailTemplatesPanel.jsx`.

Live-tested: 6th attempt → 429. (gotcha #144 = the `api.js` login-401 inline-error fix that makes it visible)

An admin (Jake-only) clicks **"Send Email to Change Payment Method"** on any client/member/specialist Payments tab → a Gmail drafts a `/update-card?token=` link → the person enters a new card/bank on Stripe's hosted `mode:'setup'` page → a webhook saves it as the engagement's default so the NEXT off-session charge uses it (no raw card data is ever seen). Work:
  1. **Capture flow** — 3 new `actions/payments/` actions: `payments_send_card_update` (AUTH/**superadmin**; enumerates a payer's reusable engagements via `card-update-shared.ts`, mints a `card_update_tokens` row, drafts the email from editable template `PAYMENTS/card_update`), `payments_loadcardupdate` + `payments_cardupdate_checkout` (PUBLIC, back the `/update-card` page). NEW `router/webhooks.ts` branch: `checkout.session.completed` + `mode==='setup'` + `metadata.payment_kind==='card_update'` → sets customer `invoice_settings.default_payment_method` + the row's `default_payment_method_id`/`payment_method_type`/`acct_last4` (+ specialist subscription default PM).
  2. **Charge paths prefer the saved PM** — `contract-chargescheduled-sweep.ts` (MAP 1 quarterly) + `tax/charge-implementation.ts` use `default_payment_method_id` IF set, else EXACT prior behavior (sweep first-PM / retainer-PI expand); specialist license already keys off its subscription default PM. **Per-engagement Stripe customers** (each engagement = its own customer; a member paying on-behalf has one customer per client), so a card-update is per-engagement and the `/update-card` page lists each.
  3. **Per-installment fee/method/last4** (gotcha #131, supersedes #128) — on a method switch the webhook FREEZES already-paid installments' `pay{N}_method` + `pay{N}_last4` before switching; `normalize.ts buildMap1Rows` computes fee/method/last4 per-installment (frozen for paid, current for scheduled) via new `cardFeeFor(base)`. A past ACH installment stays fee-free + shows ••6789; scheduled installments reflect the new card.
  4. **MAP 1 two-stage email parity** (gotcha #132) — installments 2-4 now get a confirmation at charge (sweep chains `automation_CONTRACT_confirmationemail` with `payment_number`) + a receipt on clear, like payment 1; the sweep stamps `pay{N}_status` (card→succeeded, ACH→processing) immediately so the tab shows the real state not "scheduled"; the subsequent-receipt ACH `[PROCESSING_TIME]` reworded ("allow 2-4 days" → "has now cleared", since it only sends post-clear).
  5. **RFC2047 subject encoding** (gotcha #133) — `utils/gmail-draft.ts` encodes non-ASCII subjects (fixed em-dash mojibake `â€"`).

- **Payment-failure alerting — every money-movement failure now routes to Jake's bell (full payment audit + Track 1)** (prior session, branch `claude/suspicious-mahavira-6a6727`, backend live **v465**; **NO frontend, NO DB/migrations** — backend code only; **nothing committed yet**; **VERIFIED live** via signed synthetic sandbox events). Followed a full audit of all 6 pay surfaces (MAP 1, Tax prog 1+4, Advisor, Accountant, PIP, Specialist bg+license): off-session/scheduled-charge failures were already covered, but **async failures were silent** — bounced ACH first-payments stuck at `processing` across every pipeline, `payment_intent.payment_failed` gated to Specialist only, and disputes / refund-failures / subscription-lapses entirely unhandled. All changes additive in `router/webhooks.ts` + `utils/`:
  1. **New `checkout.session.async_payment_failed`** — ACH first-payment bounce, all pipelines, via new `utils/resolve-stripe-failure.ts` (customer+metadata → pipeline/row/status-col/admin-link cascade, mirrors `checkout.session.completed`); flips the row's first-payment status to `failed` + alerts Jake (the Tax alert flags the premature "paid" email that fired at the processing stage).
  2. **Broadened `payment_intent.payment_failed`** beyond Specialist to all first-payment pipelines (skips off-session installments — `metadata.payment_number` / `payment_kind='implementation'` — which the charge sweeps already own).
  3. **`customer.subscription.updated`/`deleted`** — Specialist license past_due/canceled → "consider revoking access" alert (routed by `lic_subscription_id`); auto-clears + restores status on return to active.
  4. **`charge.dispute.created`/`closed`** — chargeback alert (action-required), cleared on close (close posts the won/lost outcome AFTER clearing so it stays visible — gotcha #122).
  5. **`charge.refunded` + `charge.refund.updated`/`refund.updated`/`refund.failed`** — tracks every refund (incl. Stripe-Dashboard-issued) + action on failed refunds.
  6. **`transfer.reversed`** — rev-share clawback alert.
  7. **Catch-all `console.log("Stripe webhook event:", type)`** so no event arrives unseen.
  8. **`utils/notify-jake-failure.ts`** gained an `actionRequired` flag (non-dismissible) + `clearJakeFailure(title)` + `clearJakeFailuresContaining(substr)`. **Policy:** action-required + auto-clear ONLY where a Stripe event reliably resolves it (rev-share ×3 [clears on retry success], license [clears on invoice.paid/active], disputes [clears on close]); everything else (ACH-bounce / PI-failed / refund-fail / transfer-reversed / bg / quarterly / impl) stays a dismissible FYI — non-dismissible without an auto-clear would be permanent clutter. The 3 rev-share handlers + license dunning were upgraded to action-required + clear-on-success.
  - **Decision: Jake-only routing** (kept ALL existing Tracy/admin/PF alerts; added Jake everywhere there was a gap — additive). **Config dependency (user did it this session):** the Stripe webhook ENDPOINT must subscribe to the new event types (both live + sandbox). **Verified** by firing signed synthetic sandbox events for every branch — action-required policy correct, mapped resolver flipped `bg_payment_status` processing→failed, subscription lapse→recover cleared the alert, dispute-close cleared the opened alert + kept the outcome visible; all test data purged. **Track 2 (per-person payment tabs) + Track 3 (payment-method change) deferred to later chats** — see the `project-payment-failure-audit-and-tabs` memory.

Yes/No** at the end → 4 conditional required tax-risk questions (`tax_general_risks`/`tax_risk_history`/`tax_worst_case`/`tax_precautions`), all stored in `sif_data` jsonb (no schema change). **Reviewer notes step** (Stage 2, after the final rev-share proposal submit): non-dismissible action notifications to **Tracy** (general notes, always) + **Tim** (`tgacsy@elitert.com`, tax-risk notes, only if tax specialist) that clear when the notes save (stored as `specialist_onboarding_progress` `tracy_general_notes`/`tim_tax_risk_notes`; fire/clear logic in `save-progress.ts`). **Stage 2 "Initial executive approval" = account-scoped two-round voting** (`automation_SPECIALIST_execvote`, AUTH): Anton (`aanderson@elitert.com`) + Paul (`platham@elitert.com`) each vote Approved / Further Questions (round 1); a vote's value is **redacted server-side in `load.ts` until BOTH vote that round** (only the caster sees their own); Further Questions opens round 2 (Approved / Denied); both-Approved (either round) → auto-draft `SPECIALIST_step3` + **auto-advance to Stage 3**; both-Denied → `automation_SPECIALIST_deniedemail` (`SPECIALIST_denied`) + `status='stopped'`; round-2 split → reset + re-vote notif. Votes table gained a **`vote_round`** column (unique now `(onboarding_id, stage, voter_name, vote_round)`). **Stage 3 background-check payment chain**: `step3-email.ts` (on approve — creates Stripe customer + `bg_checkout_token` + `further_questions_token`, drafts `SPECIALIST_step3` with Core $350 / Max $950 / Further-Questions buttons), `bg-load-payment.ts` + `bg-checkout.ts` (PUBLIC token page `/specialist-pay?token=&type=core|max` — ACH/Card Stripe Checkout one-time, metadata `pipeline=SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING`/`payment_kind=background_check`/`bg_type`), webhook branch (card → immediate; ACH → on `payment_intent.succeeded`) → `bg-confirmation.ts` (`SPECIALIST_bg_confirmation|card`/`|ach`, MAP-1-style wording, fires for both methods at payment time) + `bg-receipt.ts` (on clearance — generates **invoice + receipt PDFs** `INV-SPEC-{id}-{seq}` / `REC-SPEC-{id}-{seq}` via `specialist-html-templates.ts`, archives to Drive **`VFO Specialist Onboarding/<Name> - Specialist`** under parent `13eFhVvCgABtRgRbg0l4s2sUilOzMjrWb`, drafts `SPECIALIST_bg_receipt` [TBD body] with both attached, marks paid, FYI to Tracy, sets Core→Checkr / Max→Scherzer label). **Further Questions flow**: `/specialist-questions?token=` (`questions-request.ts`, PUBLIC) records the request + non-dismissible Tracy notif → admin "Further Questions" sub-step in Stage 3 with **Proceed** (re-send `SPECIALIST_step3_proceed`) / **Stop** (`SPECIALIST_no` + stop) buttons (`questions-resolve.ts`, AUTH) that clear Tracy's notif. **Automation Panel**: `SpecialistAutomationPanel.jsx` (Automation tab → "Specialist Onboarding"; loader `automation_load_specialist_pipelines`, AUTH) — per-specialist stage badge + StepCards (SIF, Stage 2 meetings w/ per-meeting rev-share tracking, Executive Approval w/ per-exec per-round votes, Stage 3 email, Further Questions, Payment, Invoice & Receipt) + working `SandboxModeToggle`. **Reminder sweep** (`automation_SPECIALIST_sweep`, PUBLIC service-role, cron `specialist-sweep-daily` 07:00 UTC): 48h reminder email / 96h Tracy FYI for 4 stalls — SIF not submitted, rev-share proposal unanswered (per meeting), exec hasn't voted R1/R2 (emails only the missing exec(s), no button), Core/Max/Questions not chosen. Templates `SPECIALIST_sif_reminder`/`_revshare_reminder`/`_vote_reminder`/`_choice_reminder`. Shared helpers `utils/gmail-draft.ts` + `constants/specialist-execs.ts`. **`pipeline_sandbox_config` now HAS a `SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING` row** (added this session; currently `sandbox_mode=true` for testing — must flip to live before real specialist payments). NotificationBell.jsx gained click-clears-FYI + nonce-renavigation + a `vfo:notifications-changed` event listener (global improvements). New routes `/specialist-pay`, `/specialist-questions`; new skeletons `SpecialistOnboardingListSkeleton`/`SpecialistOnboardingDetailSkeleton`. See [docs/flows/specialist-onboarding.md](specialist-onboarding.md).
- **Specialist Onboarding Step 3 (Due Diligence) + receipt-email revamp** (2026-06-04, backend live; frontend now deployed). Built out the previously-TBD `SPECIALIST_bg_receipt` Step 3 receipt email (template id 80) — carries the DD Checklist + Request-Help buttons, the 5-step process diagram, and the revenue-share proposal (Exhibit A) with Happy/Further-Questions buttons; `bg-receipt.ts` now mints `ddc_token` + `rev_share_final_token`. New **public DDC form** `/specialist-ddc` (`SpecialistDdcPage.jsx`) with file uploads to the **private `specialist-dd-materials`** bucket via signed upload URLs, save-progress + resume + submit; plus `/specialist-ddc-help` + `/specialist-revshare-final` confirmation pages. **DD review loop** (admin Stage 3): submit → Approved / Denied(reason → `SPECIALIST_ddc_edits` email) → resubmit, with a **paper trail** (`specialist_onboarding.ddc_review_log` jsonb appended by submit/approve/deny). Admin **"View progress"** expander (inside the Checklist progress box) shows every text answer + signed-URL file downloads (`automation_SPECIALIST_ddcdownload`, 300s links, path prefix-checked against onboarding id). **Stage 3 admin tracker fully restructured** (`SpecialistOnboarding.jsx`): Background Check / Due Diligence Checklist / Final Revenue Share Proposal sections + a "Stage 3 progress" rollup (green/grey/red bullets); the Core/Max "further questions" step is a separate "Questions answered / Stop" bullet that re-sends `SPECIALIST_step3_proceed` and **stays as a paper trail**. **Stage 2 per-meeting rev-share removed** ("Discuss revenue share" is now a plain checkbox; `stage2-email.ts` no longer carries the proposal/buttons); the specialist's rev-share sign-off moved to the Step 3 email, and Tracy finalizes (Confirm/Edit) via `automation_SPECIALIST_revsharefinalize` — **edits stored in `rev_share_final_text` so the original `rev_share_prepared` is preserved (View proposal shows Original + Edited; execs' View details keeps the original they voted on)**. **AI-PC / Proactive Coordinator footer** added to all 10 specialist emails (shared `utils/gmail-draft.ts` `VFO_SIGNATURE` + 3 inline: prelim/stage2/denied). Exec-vote notification (`exec-vote.ts`) now clears the **caster's OWN** vote notification immediately (was only on the both-voted tally). All action-required notifications (further-questions, DDC help, rev-share Further-Questions) are non-dismissible + title-matched so they persist until resolved and don't clear early. **10 new handlers** (`automation_SPECIALIST_loadddc`/`saveddc`/`ddcuploadurl`/`submitddc`/`ddchelp`/`revsharefinal` PUBLIC token; `ddcapprove`/`ddcedits`/`revsharefinalize`/`ddcdownload` AUTH admin); **removed** the orphaned `automation_SPECIALIST_revsharedecide` handler + `/specialist-revshare-decide` page (dead after per-meeting rev-share dropped). New `email_templates`: `SPECIALIST_ddc_edits` (id 88) + `SPECIALIST_ddc_approved` (id 87, **body still TBD — fires on "DD Checklist Approved"; fill next chat**). New columns on `specialist_onboarding` (4 migrations): `ddc_token`/`ddc_data`/`ddc_email_sent_at`/`ddc_submitted_at`/`ddc_help_requested_at`/`ddc_reminder_sent_at`/`ddc_pf_notified_at`/`ddc_review_status`/`ddc_edits_reason`/`ddc_approved_at`/`ddc_edits_email_sent_at`/`ddc_review_log`/`rev_share_final_token`/`rev_share_final_response`/`rev_share_final_response_at`/`rev_share_final_text`. New routes `/specialist-ddc`, `/specialist-ddc-help`, `/specialist-revshare-final` (removed `/specialist-revshare-decide`). See [docs/flows/specialist-onboarding.md](specialist-onboarding.md).
- **Specialist Stage-3 completion emails + Stage-4 final exec approval** (2026-06-04, branch `claude/epic-robinson-b8609d`, backend **v394** + frontend deployed, **NOT committed**). (1) **DDC form rework** (`SpecialistDdcPage.jsx`): sectioned (Materials / Credentials & Experience / **Compliance & Risk — tax specialists only** / Client Experience), per-question cards, file-only vs text-only vs file+text inputs, PLR/TOL/legal-DD as 3 fields under a "Provide one or more of the following:" subheading, **nothing required** (Tracy denies+re-sends). Admin "Checklist progress" regrouped to mirror the form (sections + question order; "View answers" toggle at top). (2) **Three Stage-3 completion emails** via NEW shared helper `utils/specialist-stage3-emails.ts` → `sendStage3CompletionEmail`: `SPECIALIST_bg_passed` (id 89), `SPECIALIST_ddc_approved` (id 87 — **now written, no longer TBD**), `SPECIALIST_revshare_complete` (id 90); each renders a blue "Step 3 progress" card (green done-pill Passed/Complete/Finalized, orange Pending) + all-clear-or-waiting line; idempotent via `<item>_complete_emailed` stage-3 progress markers; fired from `save-progress.ts` (bg passed) / `ddc-approve.ts` / `revshare-final.ts` (specialist Happy) / `revshare-finalize.ts`. (3) **All-three Stage-3→4 gate** (`maybeAdvanceStage3` in the helper): advances ONLY when bg passed + ddc approved + rev finalized (was bg+ddc); also fires the Stage-4 Tracy/Tim notes reminders on entering Stage 4. (4) **Stage 4 final exec approval now matches Stage 2 exactly**: reviewer notes (Tracy + Tim-if-tax) → 2-round account-scoped voting (`exec-vote.ts` stage-aware via `body.stage`; `load.ts` redacts stages 2 AND 4; `save-progress.ts` opens voting per stage) → "View details" (bg+type, DD checklist + file downloads, final rev share); both-Denied → stop + decline; both-Approved **auto-sends the Specialist Onboarding Agreement** (wired this session — see the Stage-4 license-flow bullet below). (5) **Stage 4 cleanup**: removed the Personal-folder + Details&benefits blocks + the legacy advance button; replaced the 3 old status lines with **7 AutoSteps** (Agreement sent / signed by specialist / signed by CEO / Payment link sent / Payment made / Confirmation email sent / Invoice/receipt sent) — **now driven** by the Stage-4 license flow below (webhook chains mark them; first `invoice.paid` advances Stage 4→5). Also fixed a bug where Stage-4 "Further Questions" stamped `vote_r2_opened_at` and triggered phantom Stage-2 sweep reminders (now Stage-2-only). See [docs/flows/specialist-onboarding.md](specialist-onboarding.md).
- **Specialist Onboarding Stage-4 agreement + monthly-license flow** (this session, branch `claude/keen-stonebraker-cacda8`, backend live **v404** + `boldsign-webhook` **v39**; ⚠️ **frontend NOT deployed** — the Stage-4 auto-send UI, the `/specialist-pay?kind=license` page, and the Stage-2/4 reviewer-notes changes live ONLY on the dev server; the email's pay button points at production and 404s until `npm run deploy`. **NOTHING committed in either repo.**). Mirrors the MAP 1 / Advisor sign-pay flow with two new pieces: the agreement's `[PROPOSED_REVENUE_SHARE]` placeholder is filled from `rev_share_final_text` || the Stage-2 `rev_share_prepared` notes before rendering, and the $99/mo license fee is a **Stripe SUBSCRIPTION** — the **first `mode=subscription` use anywhere in the codebase**. **Trigger graph:** Stage-4 both-exec-Approved → `exec-vote.ts` `applyApproval(4)` auto-chains `automation_SPECIALIST_sendagreement` (backgrounded via `EdgeRuntime.waitUntil` so the voter's request returns under the 20s frontend timeout; idempotent on `agreement_sent_at`) → renders `agreement_templates` id=12 HTML→PDF + **coordinate-based BoldSign send** (field_map: `specialist_address` p1, `specialist_signature`/`specialist_print_name`/`specialist_date` + `ert_signature`/`ert_date` p7; signer 1 = specialist, 2 = Anton; BrandId sandbox `6ca78a6b…` / live `f6b2e092…`) → `boldsign-webhook` **specialist branch** (after accountant, keyed on `lic_boldsign_document_id`): `Signed`(specialist) → `agreement_signed_by_specialist_at` + chain `automation_SPECIALIST_ceocountersign`; `Completed`/`Signed`(Anton) → `agreement_signed_by_ceo_at` + chain `automation_SPECIALIST_licstripecustomer` → reuses `bg_stripe_customer_id`, mints `lic_checkout_token`, chains `licpaymentemail` (`/specialist-pay?token=&kind=license`) → `licloadpayment` / `liccheckout` (Stripe Checkout **mode=subscription**, inline monthly $99 `price_data`, card grossed-up 2.9%+$0.30 / ACH flat, `subscription_data[metadata]` + session `metadata` `pipeline=SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING`/`payment_kind=license`/`payment_method_type`). **Webhook (`router/webhooks.ts`, 3 new additive blocks):** `checkout.session.completed` (mode=subscription + license) records subscription/method + marks `payment_made` + chains `licconfirmation`; **`invoice.paid`/`invoice.payment_succeeded`** (routed by `lic_stripe_customer_id`) → captures the payment method **if not already set** (race-proof — either event can land first), sets `lic_last_invoice_id` (per-invoice idempotency), first invoice (`billing_reason=subscription_create`) → `licconfirmation` + `licinvoicereceipt` (INV/REC-`SPECLIC` PDFs to the same Drive folder) + advance **Stage 4→5**, recurring → monthly invoice/receipt; **`invoice.payment_failed`** → Tracy dunning FYI + `lic_payment_status='failed'`. 20 new `specialist_onboarding` columns (`lic_*` + `agreement_*` + `rev_share_agreement_text` + 6 Stage-4 reminder guards — see [tables/specialists.md](specialists.md)). **Reminder sweep now 7 stalls** (removed the dead per-meeting rev-share stall #2 + `revButtons` + `SPECIALIST_revshare_reminder` template; added **DD-checklist / final-rev-share / agreement-signature / license-payment**, each 48h reminder + 96h Tracy FYI; the signature reminder fetches a fresh BoldSign embedded link). **Stage-2 reviewer notes reworked**: order is now **rev-share proposal → reviewer notes → vote**, notes are **always-visible** (shared `ReviewerNotesSection` so Stage 2 and Stage 4 mirror), **Tim shown unless tax=No** (labeled "if applicable"); `save-progress.ts` reviewer-notes notification idempotency now matches **title + unread** (multiple non-dismissible notifs can share a link), and skips a reviewer who already filled their notes; Tracy gets a non-dismissible **"submit the revenue share proposal"** action (fired by `stage2-email.ts` when all Stage-2 items are complete, cleared on submit). 10 new email templates (91–100; see Templates). `pipeline_sandbox_config` SPECIALIST_ONBOARDING still `sandbox_mode=true`. See [docs/flows/specialist-onboarding.md](specialist-onboarding.md).
- **Partnership Fast Track (PFT) automation** (this session, branch `claude/objective-hopper-26d8a3`, backend live **v415**; ⚠️ **frontend NOT deployed** — PFT track UI, `/pft-ft-decide` + `/pft-discovery` pages, the onboarding deep-link + "Accountant Type" changes live only on the dev server until `npm run deploy`. **NOTHING committed in either repo.**). Makes the previously-inert PFT engagement track ("PFT Engagement Process" tab on a Partnership-Fast-Track client; `components/admin/pft/PFTEngagementTrack.jsx`) actually send emails + drive the flow. Pipeline label `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK`; per-accountant state in NEW table `pft_engagement` (client_id uniq + `discovery_token`/`_data`/`_submitted_at`/`_email_sent_at`/`_reminder_sent_at`/`_pf_notified_at` + `ft_response_token`/`_email_sent_at`/`_response`/`_response_at`/`_reminder_sent_at`/`_pf_notified_at` + `accountant_onboarding_id`). Track structure is DB-driven (`program_client_phases`/`program_client_tasks`, `track_type='partnership_fast_track'`) — this session stripped "Phase N -" from phase names, renamed task51→"Meeting 1 confirmation email", deleted the date-only Meeting 2/3 tasks, added a "Does the Accountant need a third meeting?" Yes/No gate (both green) + a no-third-meeting decision task, and blanked both Phase 6 task lists (rendered dynamically). **Meeting confirmation** (Meeting 1/2/3) = one warm `PFT_meeting_confirm` template (placeholders `[PRIOR_MEETING]`/`[NEXT_MEETING]`/`[NEXT_MEETING_TITLE]`/`[FORM_SECTION]`/`[CLOSING]`; discovery-form button only on Meeting 2) via a 3-button decision (with date / date-not-confirmed / declined → `automation_PFT_meetingemail`, Gmail draft). **Decision step** (VFO FT / VFO Associate / No → `automation_PFT_decisionemail`): all three draft an email; FT + Associate **immediately create an `accountant_onboarding` handoff** (FT: `selected_vfo_ft`+`accountant_type='VFO FT'`; Associate: `selected_pft`+`accountant_type='VFO Associate'`); No drafts decline + sets client `status='lost'`. The **VFO FT email carries two recipient buttons** ("I Don't Need Another Meeting - Confirm Onboarding" / "I'd Like Another Meeting" → `/pft-ft-decide?token=&decision=confirm|another_meeting` → `automation_PFT_ftresponse`); confirm auto-checks the Accountant Onboarding Preliminary Meeting step as **"Request no meeting"**. **Discovery form** = public `/pft-discovery?token=` (`PftDiscoveryPage.jsx`, SIF-style, all required except "How long have you owned your firm?") → `automation_PFT_loaddiscovery`/`_submitdiscovery`; admin sees a "View discovery form" dropdown under the Meeting 2 step. **Notifications:** discovery completed / 4-day-not-done → assigned PF; FT response (either button) + VFO Associate pick → **assigned PF + Rachael** (`rhopson@elitert.com`); FT 4-day no-response → PF only. PF routing via name→email map (Evan `eanderson@`, Bridger `bsilvester@`, Ian `iwelham@`); falls back to all-admins if no Assigned PF set. **Reminders** (cron `pft-sweep-daily` 08:00 UTC): discovery 2-day reminder email to accountant + 4-day PF notice; FT 2-day reminder email (`PFT_decision_vfo_ft_reminder`) + 4-day PF notice. **Accountant Onboarding** gained an **Accountant Type** field on "+ New Onboarding" (VFO FT / VFO Associate) — associates **skip Stages 1 & 2** (no agreement/payment), go straight to Stage 3 (the `create-member` `invoice_sent_at` gate is bypassed for `accountant_type='VFO Associate'`); new `accountant_onboarding.accountant_type` column + `prelim_meeting_status` value "Request no meeting". **Onboarding notifications now deep-link** to the specific record via `?onboarding=<id>` (AccountantOnboarding + AdvisorOnboarding now read it; Specialist already did) — applied to PFT, ceo-countersign, sweep, and client-decision links for accountant + advisor. "Stage N -" prefix removed from the 3 stage titles + list-row pills in Accountant + Advisor onboarding. New skeleton `PFTTrackSkeleton`. `pipeline_sandbox_config` has a `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK` row (`sandbox_mode=true`, sandbox_email `jlatham@elitert.com`). 8 email templates (pipeline `PARTNERSHIP_FAST_TRACK`) + listed in the Email Templates tab. New routes `/pft-ft-decide`, `/pft-discovery`. See [docs/flows/partnership-fast-track.md](partnership-fast-track.md).

- **`automation_CONTRACT_revshare_sweep`** — `actions/pipeline/contract-revshare-sweep.ts` (PUBLIC, service-role auth). Trigger: Daily 02:00 UTC `pg_cron`. Trigger: Tax 2 / Tax 3 / Tax 4 / Tax 5 task buttons + 4 token-page client decisions + BoldSign + Stripe (retainer + implementation kinds) webhooks + admin buttons in TaxAutomationPanel + `tax-revshare-sweep-daily` cron driving Tax 4/5 timer transitions + Setup-phase Deposit Paid PI capture + Stop-path deposit refund. **All phases 0–7 complete + Tax 4 client-email/timer redesign + Tax 5 client-email/timer redesign + Tax 3 48h/96h reminder system + per-row dismissible Done button on notifications + program_id=4 alignment (byte-equivalent to Holistic Tax Priorities) + program-aware client labels (invoice/receipt/Stripe/BoldSign switch between "VFO Holistic Planning" and "VFO Tax Planning" via `utils/program-label.ts`) + green "Continue now"/"Proceed now" buttons on Tax 4/5 client emails (skip 24h, write `'Confirmed'` for audit) + Setup-phase deposit refund (`automation_TAX_depositrefund` + `tax_save_deposit_pi`) + sweep retry-only fix (no longer auto-starts revshare on paid plans before the Tax 4 click).** See [docs/flows/tax-planning.md](C:/vfo-react/docs/flows/tax-planning.md) for the canonical end-to-end trace and [docs/tables/tax.md](C:/vfo-react/docs/tables/tax.md) for the `client_tax_plans` column dictionary (83 columns incl. 6 `deposit_*` columns + 4 member-presentation step columns: `member_presentation_link`/`presentation_send_date`/`presentation_scheduled_at`/`presentation_email_sent_at`).
- **Advisor Onboarding automation** (17 handlers in `actions/advisor/*.ts` + 2 client token pages + `AdvisorOnboarding.jsx` panel + AddAdvisorForm radio + advisor list 5th column) — `actions/advisor/*.ts` (17 files: load-list, load-one, create, prelim-meeting, decision, decline-email, client-decision, send-agreement, ceo-countersign, stripe-customer, payment-email, load-payment, stripe-checkout, confirmation-email, invoice-receipt, create-member, sweep); `pages/Advisor{Decide,Pay}Page.jsx`; `components/admin/AdvisorOnboarding.jsx`; routes `/advisor-decide`, `/advisor-pay`. Trigger: Stage 1 / Stage 2 / Stage 3 task buttons + 1 token-page client decision (Yes/No) + BoldSign + Stripe (`metadata.payment_kind='onboarding'`) webhooks + admin "Create Advisor" button + `advisor-sweep-daily` cron driving 3-stall reminders + 14-day implicit-No auto-decline. **All Phases 1–6 complete**: DB skeleton + Stage 1 UI; Undecided email (with PDF attachment from public `advisor-onboarding-agreements` bucket) + decline email + token page; BoldSign agreement with dynamic checkbox plan picks (vfo_ft / pft / corporate) + countersign chain that reads plan picks back from BoldSign and computes `payment_amount` ($4,000 / $4,600 / $8,000 / $8,600); Stripe payment chain with `setup_future_usage=off_session` (card saved for 6-month renewal review — no auto-renew cron) + invoice/receipt PDFs mirroring MAP 1 layout with `Engagement Term: 6 months` + `Renewal Review` rows + global `document_numbers` counter shared with MAP1/Tax/PIP (`INV-ADV{id}-{seq:0004}` / `REC-ADV{id}-{seq:0004}`); Stage 3 Create Advisor button writing to `members` (`member_type='Implementation'`, `elite_status='Active'`, `advisor_model='New Model'`, `revenue_decision='Money Mapping'`, `onboarding_id=<advisor_onboarding.id>` FK, `member_category='advisor'`, member_number from the category-relative helper `nextMemberNumber('advisor','New Model')` — gotcha #48, no fixed 60100 start); 05:00 UTC reminder cron `automation_ADVISOR_sweep` with 3 stalls × 48h reminder + 96h PF notification + 14-day implicit-No auto-decline branch. Plan labels shown as full names everywhere ("VFO Fast Track" / "Partnership Fast Track" / "Corporate Membership"). `add_member_full` accepts + writes `advisor_model` (Legacy/New) so the manual Add Advisor form populates it too. `load_data` exposes `advisor_model` + `onboarding_id` in the merged member objects. Manual Add Advisor form now requires email, status, and revenue decision (status + revenue default to "-- Select --"); Advisor Onboarding new-onboarding form requires email (all frontend-only gates — backend still accepts without). See `ADVISOR_ONBOARDING_RESUMPTION.md` (repo root) for the full state-of-the-work doc.
- **Accountant Onboarding automation** (21 handlers in `actions/accountant/*.ts` + 2 client token pages + `AccountantOnboarding.jsx` panel + `AccountantAutomationPanel.jsx` + shared `MemberDirectoryView` extraction in `MembersPanel.jsx`) — `actions/accountant/*.ts` (21 files mirroring advisor's 17 + 4 new for partnership/login-setup-token: load-list, load-one, create, prelim-meeting, save-partnership, decision, decline-email, client-decision, send-agreement, ceo-countersign, stripe-customer, payment-email, load-payment, stripe-checkout, confirmation-email, invoice-receipt, create-member, sweep, login-setup-email, load-login-setup, submit-login-setup, automation-load-pipelines); `pages/Accountant{Decide,Pay}Page.jsx`; `components/admin/AccountantOnboarding.jsx`; `components/admin/AccountantAutomationPanel.jsx`; routes `/accountant-decide`, `/accountant-pay`. Trigger: Stage 1 (incl. new **Partnership?** step between Prelim Meeting and Decision) / Stage 2 / Stage 3 task buttons + 1 token-page client decision (Yes/No) + BoldSign + Stripe (`metadata.pipeline='ACCOUNTANT_ONBOARDING'` + `metadata.payment_kind='onboarding'`) webhooks + admin "Create Accountant & Send Setup Link" button + `accountant-sweep-daily` cron driving 3-stall reminders + 14-day implicit-No auto-decline. **Cloned from advisor with these differences:** (a) new `accountant_partnership` TEXT column on `accountant_onboarding` (values `'No accountant partnership'` or `'Accountant Partnership'`) set in the Stage 1 Partnership? step, gates Decision buttons until picked; (b) `agreement_templates` has TWO rows for accountant (one per partnership branch) — `send-agreement.ts` loads by `payment_plan` matching the partnership choice; (c) pricing: `vfo_ft_checkbox` is required, costs $4,000 (No partnership) or $2,000 (Accountant Partnership); `corporate_checkbox` optional +$600 either way; `pft_checkbox` removed entirely; no FormGroups validation; (d) `ceo-countersign.ts` reads partnership from row to compute price; (e) `create-member.ts` writes `member_type='Implementation'`, `elite_status='Active'`, `advisor_model='New Model'`, `accountant_onboarding_id=<id>` FK — NO `revenue_decision` (accountants don't have a revenue decision); (f) member_number from the category-relative helper `nextMemberNumber('accountant', <model>)` + `member_category='accountant'` (gotcha #48 — the old 30000/90000 ranges + accountant-only filter are gone); (g) invoice/receipt format `INV-ACC{onboarding_id}-{seq:0004}` / `REC-ACC{onboarding_id}-{seq:0004}` from `document_numbers` table (new `accountant_onboarding_id` FK column added); (h) accountant profile in admin omits Revenue Decision via shared `MemberProfile` `hiddenFields={['revenue_decision']}` prop; (i) `MemberSetupPage.jsx` updated to fall through advisor→accountant token lookup (one shared `/member-setup` page handles both pipelines). `MembersPanel.jsx` refactored: extracted `MemberDirectoryView` used by both `AdvisorsPanel` and `AccountantsPanel` (search + list + click-into-profile multi-tab); accountant filter excludes advisor-onboarded members via `onboarding_id IS NULL` guard. Plus tightened the BoldSign field-read parser in BOTH `advisor/ceo-countersign.ts` and `accountant/ceo-countersign.ts` (latent advisor bug: `s.order === 1` never matched; permissive lookup now tries `signerOrder` / `order` / `signer_order` + falls back to `signerDetails[0]`; multi-key field match by `id` / `fieldId` / `name` / `label`; multi-value truthy by `value` / `checked` / `selected`; soft-fail with admin notification + diagnostic shape dump if read fails — Anton's countersign email always sends). RLS now ENABLED on both `advisor_onboarding` and `accountant_onboarding` (service-role only; no policies since all handlers use service-role).
- **PIP Meetings automation** (14 handlers in `actions/msm/` + 1 new util + 4 frontend surfaces) — `actions/msm/*.ts` (14 PIP files: load-pip-phases, add-pip-meetings-for-year, update-pip-meeting, save-pip-purchase, pip-meeting-confirmation-email, automation-load-pip-pipelines [6 AUTH]; pip-stripe-customer, pip-payment-email, pip-load-payment, pip-stripe-checkout, pip-confirmation-email, pip-invoice-receipt, pip-revshare [7 PUBLIC chain-callable]) + `utils/pip-html-templates.ts` (generatePipInvoiceHTML + generatePipReceiptHTML); plus extension to `msm_save_priority_task` to persist `notes`. Frontend: `components/admin/pip/PipMeetingsTab.jsx` (4th tab on Holistic clients, year-grouped meeting list, "+ Add Year" button, locked-meeting styling), `components/admin/pip/PipPurchaseDecisionForm.jsx` (3-decision dropdown + Pricing + Revenue Split per branch), `components/admin/PipAutomationPanel.jsx` (admin Automation Panel section, one row per PIP purchase, 5 collapsible step sections mirroring MAP 1), `pages/PipPayPage.jsx` (`/pip-pay` route, ACH/Card picker clone of `/pay`). State on `client_priority_tracks` (`track_type='pip'`) with 25 new `pip_*` columns: `pip_engagement_year`, `pip_scheduled_date`, `pip_completed_date`, `pip_paid`, `pip_purchase_kind` ('tax_planning'|'additional_pip'), `pip_purchase_pip_count`, `pip_purchase_gross`, `pip_purchase_member_contribution`, `pip_purchase_amount`, `pip_purchase_member_share`, `pip_purchase_vfos_share`, `pip_stripe_customer_id`, `pip_checkout_token`, `pip_payment_intent_id`, `pip_payment_status`, `pip_payment_method_type`, `pip_acct_last4`, `pip_card_processing_fee`, `pip_payment_completed_at`, `pip_payment_email_sent_at`, `pip_confirmation_email_sent_at`, `pip_invoice_number`, `pip_receipt_number`, `pip_invoice_drive_id`, `pip_receipt_drive_id`, `pip_invoice_receipt_email_sent_at`, `pip_rev_share_status`, `pip_rev_share_transfer_id`, `pip_rev_share_amount`, `pip_rev_share_completed_at`, `pip_rev_member_email_sent_at`, `pip_purchased_from_track_id` (FK back to source meeting for locked child meetings). Trigger graph: admin opens Holistic client's PIP Meetings tab → "+ Add Year" inserts N tracks (`pip_paid=true`) → admin clicks a meeting → Phase 3 "Purchase Additional Services (optional)" form submit (decision=No: no chain; decision=Tax Planning OR Additional PIP: fires `automation_PIP_stripecustomer` → `_paymentemail` chain; Additional PIP also creates N locked child meetings via `msm_add_pip_meetings_for_year(paid=false, source_track_id=X)`) → client clicks `/pip-pay?token=` → `_loadpayment` + `_stripecheckout` → Stripe Checkout (one-time only, NO `setup_future_usage`) → Stripe webhook routes by `metadata.pipeline='PIP'` + `metadata.payment_kind='purchase'` → cascade fallback after MAP1/Tax/Advisor → `_confirmationemail` + `_invoicereceipt` (PDFs uploaded to client's Drive folder, `INV-PIP-{clientRef}-{seq:0004}` / `REC-PIP-{clientRef}-{seq:0004}` from shared global `document_numbers` counter) + `_revshare` (Stripe Connect transfer for Revenue Share members; Money Mapping for MM members; same banner styling as MAP1/Tax). On Additional PIP success, revshare also unlocks locked child meetings (`UPDATE client_priority_tracks SET pip_paid=true WHERE pip_purchased_from_track_id=<source>`). **No Tracy's-sheet verification** — `pip_purchase_member_share`/`pip_purchase_vfos_share` are admin-entered at form-submit and trusted as-is. **No retry sweep** — failed Stripe Connect transfers need manual re-fire via service-role. 4 email templates (pipeline='PIP'): `PIP_meeting_confirmation`, `PIP_payment`, `PIP_confirmation`, `PIP_invoicereceipt_email`. See [docs/flows/pip-meetings.md](C:/vfo-react/docs/flows/pip-meetings.md) for the canonical end-to-end trace.
